local _genv = (type(getgenv) == 'function' and getgenv()) or _G
if type(getgenv) ~= 'function' then
    getgenv = function() return _G end
end
if type(hookfunction) ~= 'function' then
    hookfunction = function(a, b) pcall(function() end) end
end
if type(setreadonly) ~= 'function' then
    setreadonly = function(t, v) end
end
if type(getrawmetatable) ~= 'function' then
    getrawmetatable = function(t) return getmetatable(t) end
end
if type(newcclosure) ~= 'function' then
    newcclosure = function(f) return f end
end
if type(getnamecallmethod) ~= 'function' then
    getnamecallmethod = function() return "" end
end
if type(getgc) ~= 'function' then
    getgc = function() return {} end
end
if type(getupvalues) ~= 'function' then
    getupvalues = function(f) return {} end
end
if type(sethiddenproperty) ~= 'function' then
    sethiddenproperty = function(obj, prop, val)
        pcall(function() obj[prop] = val end)
    end
end
if type(fireproximityprompt) ~= 'function' then
    fireproximityprompt = function(prompt, dist)
        pcall(function()
            local plrs = game:GetService('Players')
            local char = plrs.LocalPlayer.Character
            if char and prompt and prompt:IsA('ProximityPrompt') then
                prompt.Triggered:Connect(function() end)
                game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('ProximityPrompt', prompt)
            end
        end)
    end
end
local Services = setmetatable({}, {
    __index = function(self, serviceName)
        local service = game:GetService(serviceName)
        rawset(self, serviceName, service)
        return service
    end,
})
local HttpService = Services.HttpService
local FolderName = 'SEI Hub'
local FileName = 'Settings.json'
local FullPath = FolderName .. '/' .. FileName
if makefolder and not isfolder(FolderName) then
    makefolder(FolderName)
end
_G.SaveData = _G.SaveData or {}
function SaveSettings()
    if not writefile then
        return false
    end
    local success = pcall(function()
        local json = HttpService:JSONEncode(_G.SaveData)
        writefile(FullPath, json)
    end)
    return success
end
function LoadSettings()
    if not (isfile and isfile(FullPath)) then
        return false
    end
    local success, result = pcall(function()
        local content = readfile(FullPath)
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(content)
    end)
    if success and result then
        _G.SaveData = result
        return true
    end
    return false
end
function GetSetting(name, default)
    return _G.SaveData[name] ~= nil and _G.SaveData[name] or default
end
LoadSettings()

function HasKen()
    local char = player.Character
    return char and CollectionService:HasTag(char, 'Ken')
end
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(1) 
    while _G.AutoKen do
        task.wait(0.5) 
        if not HasKen() then
            pcall(function()
                commE:FireServer('Ken', true)
            end)
        end
    end
end)

local placeId = game.PlaceId
local function IsDungeonActive()
    local map = workspace:FindFirstChild('Map')
    if map then
        if map:FindFirstChild('Dungeon') then return true end
        for _, child in pairs(map:GetChildren()) do
            if child.Name:find('Dungeon') or child.Name:find('dungeon') then return true end
        end
    end
    for _, child in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
        if child.Name:find('Dungeon') or child.Name:find('dungeon') then return true end
    end
    return false
end
local IsDungeon = IsDungeonActive()

EquipWeapon = function(I)
    if not I then
        return
    end
    if plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(I) then
        plr.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(I))
    end
end
weaponSc = function(I)
    for e, K in pairs(plr.Backpack:GetChildren())do
        if K:IsA('Tool') then
            if K.ToolTip == I then
                EquipWeapon(K.Name)
            end
        end
    end
end
pcall(function()
    if type(hookfunction) == 'function' then
        hookfunction(require((game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')).Effect.Container.Death), function() end)
        hookfunction((require((game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')):WaitForChild('GuideModule'))).ChangeDisplayedNPC, function() end)
        hookfunction(error, function() end)
        hookfunction(warn, function() end)
    end
end)

local G = {}
G.__index = G
G.Alive = function(I)
    if not I then
        return
    end
    local e = I:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
    return e and e.Health > 0
end
G.Pos = function(I, e)
    local pos = typeof(I) == 'CFrame' and I.Position or (I and I.Position or Root.Position)
    return (Root.Position - pos).Magnitude <= e
end
G.Dist = function(I, e)
    return (Root.Position - (I:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')).Position).Magnitude <= e
end
G.DistH = function(I, e)
    return (Root.Position - (I:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')).Position).Magnitude > e
end
_G.MobHeight = _G.MobHeight or 30
_G.SaveData.BringMobs_Save = true
_G.BringRadius = 200
local BringPos = nil
local _BringMobName = nil
G.Kill = function(I, e)
    if not (I and e) then return end
    local hrp = I:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = I:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    local root = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return end
    if not I:GetAttribute("Locked") then
        I:SetAttribute("Locked", hrp.CFrame)
    end
    local centerCF = I:GetAttribute("Locked")
    PosMon = centerCF.Position
    BringPos = centerCF
    _BringMobName = I.Name
    _B = true
    BringEnemy()
    EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)
    local tool = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    if not tool then return end
    _tp(centerCF * CFrame.new(0, _G.MobHeight, 0))
end
G.Kill2 = function(I, e)
    if I and e then
        if not I:GetAttribute('Locked') then
            I:SetAttribute('Locked', I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
        end
        PosMon = I.HumanoidRootPart.Position
        BringPos = I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
        _BringMobName = I.Name
        BringEnemy()
        EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)
        local e = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
        local K = e.ToolTip
        if K == 'Blox Fruit' then
            _tp((I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 10, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0))
        else
            _tp((I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 8)) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0))
        end
        if RandomCFrame then
            task.wait(0.1)
            _tp(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
            task.wait(0.1)
            _tp(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(25, 30, 0))
            task.wait(0.1)
            _tp(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
            task.wait(0.1)
            _tp(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
            task.wait(0.1)
            _tp(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
        end
    end
end
G.KillSea = function(I, e)
    if I and e then
        if not I:GetAttribute('Locked') then
            I:SetAttribute('Locked', I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
        end
        PosMon = I.HumanoidRootPart.Position
        BringPos = I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
        _BringMobName = I.Name
        BringEnemy()
        EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)
        local e = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
        local K = e.ToolTip
        if K == 'Blox Fruit' then
            _tp((I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 10, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0))
        else
            notween(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 50, 8))
            task.wait(0.85)
            notween(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 400, 0))
            task.wait(1)
        end
    end
end
G.Sword = function(I, e)
    if I and e then
        if not I:GetAttribute('Locked') then
            I:SetAttribute('Locked', I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
        end
        PosMon = I.HumanoidRootPart.Position
        BringPos = I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
        _BringMobName = I.Name
        BringEnemy()
        weaponSc('Sword')
        _tp(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
        if RandomCFrame then
            task.wait(0.1)
            _tp(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
            task.wait(0.1)
            _tp(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(25, 30, 0))
            task.wait(0.1)
            _tp(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
            task.wait(0.1)
            _tp(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
            task.wait(0.1)
            _tp(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
        end
    end
end
_G.FruitSkills = {
    Z = false,
    X = false,
    C = false,
    V = false,
    F = false,
}
UseFruitSkills = function()
    weaponSc('Blox Fruit')
    if _G.FruitSkills.Z then
        Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'Z')
    end
    if _G.FruitSkills.X then
        Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'X')
    end
    if _G.FruitSkills.C then
        Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'C')
    end
    if _G.FruitSkills.V then
        Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'V')
    end
    if _G.FruitSkills.F then
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'F', false, game)
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'F', false, game)
    end
end
G.Mas = function(I, e)
    if I and e then
        if not I:GetAttribute('Locked') then
            I:SetAttribute('Locked', I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
        end
        PosMon = I.HumanoidRootPart.Position
        BringPos = I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
        _BringMobName = I.Name
        BringEnemy()
        if I.Humanoid.Health <= HealthM then
            _tp(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0))
            UseFruitSkills()
        else
            weaponSc('Melee')
            _tp(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
        end
    end
end
G.Masgun = function(I, e)
    if I and e then
        if not I:GetAttribute('Locked') then
            I:SetAttribute('Locked', I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
        end
        PosMon = I.HumanoidRootPart.Position
        BringPos = I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
        _BringMobName = I.Name
        BringEnemy()
        if I.Humanoid.Health <= HealthM then
            _tp(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 35, 8))
            Useskills('Gun', 'Z')
            Useskills('Gun', 'X')
        else
            weaponSc('Melee')
            _tp(I.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
        end
    end
end
statsSettings = function(I, e)
    if I == 'Melee' then
        if plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
            replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AddPoint', 'Melee', e)
        end
    elseif I == 'Defense' then
        if plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
            replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AddPoint', 'Defense', e)
        end
    elseif I == 'Sword' then
        if plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
            replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AddPoint', 'Sword', e)
        end
    elseif I == 'Gun' then
        if plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
            replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AddPoint', 'Gun', e)
        end
    elseif I == 'Devil' then
        if plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
            replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AddPoint', 'Demon Fruit', e)
        end
    end
end

-- Heartbeat loop สำหรับ FastAttack: เปิดได้ปิดได้ตามปุ่ม
game:GetService('RunService').Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if _G.FastAttack or IsFarmActive() or _G.AutoBoss or _G.FarmAllBoss or _G.DungeonFarm then
        if not _G.Seriality then _G.Seriality = true end
        if not _G.NewAutoAttack then _G.NewAutoAttack = true end
    else
        if _G.Seriality then _G.Seriality = false end
        if _G.NewAutoAttack then _G.NewAutoAttack = false end
    end
end)
function IsRaidMob(mob)
    local n = mob.Name:lower()
    if n:find('raid') or n:find('microchip') or n:find('island') then
        return true
    end
    if mob:GetAttribute('IsRaid') or mob:GetAttribute('RaidMob') or mob:GetAttribute('IsBoss') then
        return true
    end
    local hum = mob:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
    if hum and hum.WalkSpeed == 0 then
        return true
    end
    if mob.Parent and tostring(mob.Parent):lower():find('_worldorigin') then
        return true
    end
    return false
end
local Http = game:GetService("HttpService")
local ServerBrowser = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("__ServerBrowser")
function Hop()
    local url = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/"..game.PlaceId.."/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"
    local success, data = pcall(function()
        return Http:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet(url))
    end)
    if not success or not data or not data.data then
        warn("Failed to fetch servers")
        return
    end
    local target = nil
    for _, server in pairs(data.data) do
        if server and server.playing and server.maxPlayers then
            if server.playing < server.maxPlayers then
                target = server
                break
            end
        end
    end
    if not target then
        warn("No server found")
        return
    end
    for i = 1, 3 do
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()
                ServerBrowser:InvokeServer("teleport", target.id)
            end)
        end)
    end
end
-- ลูปควบคุม SimulationRadius
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        if IsFarmActive() then
            pcall(function()
                sethiddenproperty(LP, 'SimulationRadius', math.huge)
            end)
        end
        task.wait(1.5)
    end
end)

BringEnemy = function()
    if not _G.SaveData.BringMobs_Save or not BringPos or not _BringMobName then return end

    pcall(function()
        sethiddenproperty(plr, 'SimulationRadius', math.huge)
    end)

    for _, mob in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
        if mob.Name == _BringMobName and mob.Parent then
            local root = mob:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
            local hum  = mob:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
            if root and hum and hum.Health > 0
                and (root.Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= (_G.BringRange or 350)
            then
                root.CFrame = BringPos
                hum.JumpPower = 0
                hum.WalkSpeed = 0
                root.Transparency = 1
                root.CanCollide = false

                local head = mob:FindFirstChild('Head')
                if head then head.CanCollide = false end

                local anim = hum:FindFirstChild('Animator')
                if anim then pcall(function() anim:Destroy() end) end

                if not root:FindFirstChild('Lock') then
                    local bv = Instance.new('BodyVelocity')
                    bv.Name = 'Lock'
                    bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e5, 1e5, 1e5)
                    bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                    bv.Parent = root
                end

                hum:ChangeState(11)
            end
        end
    end
end

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.1) do
        if IsFarmActive() and _G.SaveData.BringMobs_Save and _BringMobName then
            _B = true
            BringEnemy()
        else
            _B = false
        end
    end
end)
Useskills = function(I, e)
    -- ล็อคทิศทางตัวละครก่อนใช้สกิล เพื่อป้องกันตัวหลัง
    local hrp = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
    local savedCFrame = hrp and hrp.CFrame
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local savedCamCFrame = cam and cam.CFrame

    local function sendKey(k)
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, k, false, game)
        task.wait(0.05)
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, k, false, game)
        -- คืนทิศทางหลังกดสกิล
        if hrp and savedCFrame then
            hrp.CFrame = savedCFrame
        end
        if cam and savedCamCFrame then
            cam.CFrame = savedCamCFrame
        end
    end

    if I == 'Melee' then
        weaponSc('Melee')
        if e == 'Z' then sendKey('Z')
        elseif e == 'X' then sendKey('X')
        elseif e == 'C' then sendKey('C')
        end
    elseif I == 'Sword' then
        weaponSc('Sword')
        if e == 'Z' then sendKey('Z')
        elseif e == 'X' then sendKey('X')
        end
    elseif I == 'Blox Fruit' then
        weaponSc('Blox Fruit')
        if e == 'Z' then sendKey('Z')
        elseif e == 'X' then sendKey('X')
        elseif e == 'C' then sendKey('C')
        elseif e == 'V' then sendKey('V')
        end
    elseif I == 'Gun' then
        weaponSc('Gun')
        if e == 'Z' then sendKey('Z')
        elseif e == 'X' then sendKey('X')
        end
    end
    if I == 'nil' and e == 'Y' then sendKey('Y') end
end
pcall(function()
    if type(getrawmetatable) == 'function' and type(setreadonly) == 'function' then
        local J = getrawmetatable(game)
        local i = J.__namecall
        setreadonly(J, false)
        J.__namecall = newcclosure(function(...)
            local I = getnamecallmethod()
            local e = {...}
            if tostring(I) == 'FireServer' then
                if tostring(e[1]) == 'RemoteEvent' then
                    if tostring(e[2]) ~= 'true' and tostring(e[2]) ~= 'false' then
                        if _G.FarmMastery_G and not SoulGuitar or _G.FarmMastery_Dev or _G.FarmBlazeEM or _G.Prehis_Skills or _G.SeaBeast1 or _G.FishBoat or _G.PGB or _G.Leviathan1 or _G.Complete_Trials or _G.AimMethod and ABmethod == 'AimBots Skill' or _G.AimMethod and ABmethod == 'Auto Aimbots' then
                            e[2] = MousePos
                            return i(unpack(e))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return i(...)
        end)
    end
end)
GetConnectionEnemies = function(I)
    for e, K in pairs(replicated:GetChildren())do
        if K:IsA('Model') and ((typeof(I) == 'table' and table.find(I, K.Name) or K.Name == I) and (K:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and K.Humanoid.Health > 0)) then
            return K
        end
    end
    for e, K in next, game.Workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()do
        if K:IsA('Model') and ((typeof(I) == 'table' and table.find(I, K.Name) or K.Name == I) and (K:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and K.Humanoid.Health > 0)) then
            return K
        end
    end
end
LowCpu = function()
    local I = true
    local e = game
    local K = e.Workspace
    local n = e.Lighting
    local d = K.Terrain
    d.WaterWaveSize = 0
    d.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
    d.WaterReflectance = 0
    d.WaterTransparency = 0
    n.GlobalShadows = false
    n.FogEnd = 9000000000
    n.Brightness = 1
    local _settings = settings()
    _settings.Rendering.QualityLevel = 'Level01'
    for _idx, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants())do
        if obj:IsA('Part') or obj:IsA('Union') or obj:IsA('CornerWedgePart') or obj:IsA('TrussPart') then
            obj.Material = 'Plastic'
            obj.Reflectance = 0
        elseif obj:IsA('Decal') or obj:IsA('Texture') and I then
            obj.Transparency = 1
        elseif obj:IsA('ParticleEmitter') or obj:IsA('Trail') then
            obj.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0)
        elseif obj:IsA('Explosion') then
            obj.BlastPressure = 1
            obj.BlastRadius = 1
        elseif obj:IsA('Fire') or obj:IsA('SpotLight') or obj:IsA('Smoke') or obj:IsA('Sparkles') then
            obj.Enabled = false
        elseif obj:IsA('MeshPart') then
            obj.Material = 'Plastic'
            obj.Reflectance = 0
            obj.TextureID = ""
        end
    end
    for _idx2, child in pairs(n:GetChildren())do
        if child:IsA('BlurEffect') or child:IsA('SunRaysEffect') or child:IsA('ColorCorrectionEffect') or child:IsA('BloomEffect') or child:IsA('DepthOfFieldEffect') then
            child.Enabled = false
        end
    end
end

    end
end

CheckSeaBeast = function()
    local seaBeasts = workspace:FindFirstChild('SeaBeasts')
    if not seaBeasts then
        return false
    end
    for _, e in pairs(seaBeasts:GetChildren())do
        if e.Name ~= 'Leviathan' and e:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
            if e:FindFirstChild('Health') and e.Health.Value > 0 then
                return true
            elseif e:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and e.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                return true
            elseif not e:FindFirstChild('Health') and not e:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') then
                return true
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end
CheckLeviathan = function()
    if workspace:FindFirstChild('SeaBeasts') and workspace.SeaBeasts:FindFirstChild('Leviathan') then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function RefreshReferenceCharacter(char)
    Character = char or plr.Character
    Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')
    HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
    LocalPlayer = plr
end

    RefreshReferenceCharacter(char)
end

    RefreshReferenceCharacter(plr.Character)
end

    RefreshReferenceCharacter(char)
end)
sendKeySkill = function(skill)
    local keyCode = Enum.KeyCode[skill]
    if keyCode then
        local hrp = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
        local savedCF = hrp and hrp.CFrame
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local savedCamCF = cam and cam.CFrame
        game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, keyCode, false, game)
        task.wait(0.1)
        game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, keyCode, false, game)
        if hrp and savedCF then hrp.CFrame = savedCF end
        if cam and savedCamCF then cam.CFrame = savedCamCF end
    end
end

    return math.floor(tonumber(n) + 0.5)
end

    end

GetDistance = function(target)
    local root = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
    local pos = typeof(target) == 'CFrame' and target.Position or target and target.Position
    if root and pos then
        return math.floor((pos - root.Position).Magnitude)
    end
    return math.huge
end
InstantTp = function(value)
    notween(value)
end
TweenPlayer = function(pos)
    _tp(pos)
    return {
        Stop = function()
            shouldTween = false
        end,
    }
end
TweenPlayerOld = function(pos)
    return TweenPlayer(pos)
end
TweenBoat = function(pos)
    local boat = CheckBoat()
    if boat and boat:FindFirstChild('VehicleSeat') then
        local target = typeof(pos) == 'Instance' and pos.CFrame or pos
        local dist = (boat.VehicleSeat.Position - target.Position).Magnitude
        local tween = game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(boat.VehicleSeat, TweenInfo.new(dist / (_G.BoatTweenSpeed or _G.SetSpeedBoat or 300), Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {CFrame = target})
        tween:Play()
        return {
            Stop = function()
                tween:Cancel()
            end,
        }
    end
    return {
        Stop = function() end,
    }
end
Stop = function()
    shouldTween = false
end
StopTween = function(State)
    shouldTween = State and true or false
    _G.StopTween = not State
end
RemoveAnimation = function(Mon)
    if Mon and Mon:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') then
        for _, track in pairs(Mon.Humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks())do
            track:Stop()
        end
    end
end
function AttackModule:AttackEnemy(Target)
    if Target and G.Alive(Target) then
        G.Kill(Target, true)
    else
        Click()
    end
end
function AttackModule:AttackNearest()
    local root = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
    local nearest, dist = nil, math.huge
    if not root then
        return nil
    end
    for _, mob in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren())do
        if G.Alive(mob) and mob:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
            local d = (mob.HumanoidRootPart.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
            if d < dist then
                nearest = mob
                dist = d
            end
        end
    end
    if nearest then
        self:AttackEnemy(nearest)
    end
    return nearest
end
function AttackModule:BladeHits()
    return self:AttackNearest()
end

EquipWeaponSword = function()
    for _, tool in pairs(plr.Backpack:GetChildren())do

            EquipWeapon(tool.Name)
            return tool
        end
    end
end
getWeapon = function(weapon)
    for _, tool in pairs(plr.Backpack:GetChildren())do
        if tool:IsA('Tool') and tool.ToolTip == weapon then
            return tool.Name
        end
    end
    local charTool = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
    if charTool and charTool.ToolTip == weapon then
        return charTool.Name
    end
end
getToolTip = function()
    local tool = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
    return tool and tool.ToolTip
end

    local inv = replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory')

        end
    end
    return false
end

getHighestFloor = function()
    local dungeon = workspace:FindFirstChild('Map') and workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('Dungeon')
    local highest, floor = -math.huge, nil
    if dungeon then
        for _, v in pairs(dungeon:GetChildren())do
            local num = tonumber(v.Name)
            if num and num > highest then
                highest = num
                floor = v
            end
        end
    end
    return floor
end
getCurrentFloor = function()
    local floor = getHighestFloor()
    local dungeon = workspace:FindFirstChild('Map') and workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('Dungeon')
    local num = floor and tonumber(floor.Name)
    return dungeon and num and (dungeon:FindFirstChild(tostring(num - 2)) or floor)
end

useAllSkill = function()
    for _, category in ipairs({'Melee', 'Sword', 'Blox Fruit', 'Gun'})do
        local weapon = getWeapon(category)
        if weapon then
            EquipWeapon(weapon)
            for _, key in ipairs({'Z', 'X', 'C', 'V', 'F'})do
                sendKeySkill(key)
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
        end
    end
end
AddEsp = function(Name, Parent)
    if not Parent or Parent:FindFirstChild(Name) then
        return
    end
    local BillboardGui = Instance.new('BillboardGui')
    BillboardGui.Parent = Parent
    BillboardGui.Name = Name
    BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
    BillboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 50)
    BillboardGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
    local TextLabel = Instance.new('TextLabel')
    TextLabel.Parent = BillboardGui
    TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    TextLabel.TextSize = 15
    TextLabel.Text = Name
end
DodgeSeabeasts = function()
    local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild('SeaBeasts')
    if not folder then
        return false
    end
    for _, seaBeast in pairs(folder:GetChildren())do
        local humanoid = seaBeast:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
        local anims = seaBeast:FindFirstChild('Anims')
        local animator = humanoid and (humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass('Animator') or humanoid)
        if animator and anims then
            for _, anim in pairs(anims:GetChildren())do
                if anim:IsA('Animation') and table.find({'rbxassetid://8708221792', 'rbxassetid://8708222556', 'rbxassetid://8708223619', 'rbxassetid://8708225668'}, anim.AnimationId) then
                    for _, track in pairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks())do
                        if track.Animation.AnimationId == anim.AnimationId and track.IsPlaying then
                            return true
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end
GetCountMaterials = function(MaterialName)
    for _, item in pairs(replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory'))do
        if item.Name == MaterialName then
            return item.Count or 0
        end
    end
    return 0
end
GetHighestPoint = function()
    local island = workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('MysticIsland')
    if not island then
        return nil
    end
    for _, item in pairs(island:GetDescendants())do
        if item:IsA('MeshPart') and item.MeshId == 'rbxassetid://6745037796' then
            return item
        end
    end
end

DropFruits = function()
    for I, e in next, plr.Backpack:GetChildren()do
        if string.find(e.Name, 'Fruit') then
            EquipWeapon(e.Name)
            task.wait(0.1)
            if plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible == true then
                plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible = false
            end
            EquipWeapon(e.Name);
            (plr.Character:FindFirstChild(e.Name)).EatRemote:InvokeServer('Drop')
        end
    end
    for I, e in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren())do
        if string.find(e.Name, 'Fruit') then
            EquipWeapon(e.Name)
            task.wait(0.1)
            if plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible == true then
                plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible = false
            end
            EquipWeapon(e.Name);
            (plr.Character:FindFirstChild(e.Name)).EatRemote:InvokeServer('Drop')
        end
    end
end

GetIn = function(I)
    for e, K in pairs(replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory'))do
        if type(K) == 'table' then
            if K.Name == I or plr.Character:FindFirstChild(I) or plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(I) then
                return true
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end
GetM = function(I)
    for e, K in pairs(replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory'))do
        if type(K) == 'table' then
            if K.Type == 'Material' then
                if K.Name == I then
                    return K.Count
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return 0
end
GetWP = function(I)
    for e, K in pairs(replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory'))do
        if type(K) == 'table' then
            if K.Type == 'Sword' then
                if K.Name == I or plr.Character:FindFirstChild(I) or plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(I) then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end
getInfinity_Ability = function(I, e)
    if not Root then
        return
    end
    if I == 'Soru' and e then
        for I, K in next, getgc()do
            if plr.Character.Soru then
                if typeof(K) == 'function' and (getfenv(K)).script == plr.Character.Soru then
                    for I, K in next, getupvalues(K)do
                        if typeof(K) == 'table' then
                            repeat
                                task.wait(Sec)
                                K.LastUse = 0
                            until not e or plr.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    elseif I == 'Energy' and e then
        plr.Character.Energy.Changed:Connect(function()
            if e then
                plr.Character.Energy.Value = Energy
            end
        end)
    elseif I == 'Observation' and e then
        local I = plr.VisionRadius
        I.Value = math.huge
    end
end
Hop = function()
    pcall(function()
        for I = math.random(1, math.random(40, 75)), 100, 1 do
            local e = replicated.__ServerBrowser:InvokeServer(I)
            for I, e in next, e do
                if tonumber(e.Count) < 12 then
                    TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, I)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end
local C = Instance.new('Part', workspace)
C.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
C.Name = 'Rip_Indra'
C.Anchored = true
C.CanCollide = false
C.CanTouch = false
C.Transparency = 1
local M = workspace:FindFirstChild(C.Name)
if M and M ~= C then
    M:Destroy()
end
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(0.25) 
    while task.wait() do
        if C and C.Parent == workspace then
            if shouldTween then
                (getgenv()).OnFarm = true
            else
                (getgenv()).OnFarm = false
            end
        else
            (getgenv()).OnFarm = false
        end
    end
end)
task.spawn(function()
    local I = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    repeat
        task.wait()
    until I.Character and I.Character.PrimaryPart
    C.CFrame = I.Character.PrimaryPart.CFrame
    while task.wait() do
        pcall(function()
            if (getgenv()).OnFarm then
                if C and C.Parent == workspace then
                    local e = I.Character and I.Character.PrimaryPart
                    if e then
                        C.CFrame = e.CFrame
                    end
                end
                local e = I.Character
                if e then
                    for _, v in pairs(e:GetChildren())do
                        if v:IsA('BasePart') then
                            v.CanCollide = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            else
                local e = I.Character
                if e then
                    for _, v in pairs(e:GetChildren())do
                        if v:IsA('BasePart') then
                            v.CanCollide = true
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)
getgenv().TweenSpeedFar = 200
getgenv().TweenSpeedNear = 200
_tp = function(I)
    local e = plr.Character
    if not e or not e:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
        return
    end
    local HRP = e.HumanoidRootPart
    shouldTween = true
    getgenv().OnFarm = false
    if HRP.Anchored then
        HRP.Anchored = false
        task.wait()
    end
    if e.Humanoid.Sit == true then
        HRP.CFrame = CFrame.new(HRP.Position.X, I.Y, HRP.Position.Z)
    end
    -- เก็บ fly BodyVelocity/BodyGyro ไว้ก่อน tween
    local savedFly = {}
    if _G.Fly then
        for _, v in ipairs(HRP:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA('BodyVelocity') or v:IsA('BodyGyro') then
                savedFly[#savedFly+1] = v
                v.Parent = nil
            end
        end
    end
    local distance = (HRP.Position - I.Position).Magnitude
    local speed = getgenv().TweenSpeedFar or 370 
    local tweenTime = distance / speed
    local tween = game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(
        HRP,
        TweenInfo.new(tweenTime, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),
        {CFrame = I}
    )
    tween:Play()
    task.spawn(function()
        while tween.PlaybackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Playing do
            if not shouldTween then
                tween:Cancel()
                break
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        -- restore fly objects หลัง tween จบ
        if _G.Fly then
            for _, v in ipairs(savedFly) do
                v.Parent = HRP
            end
        end
        getgenv().OnFarm = true
    end)
end
TeleportToTarget = function(I)
    _tp(I)
end
notween = function(I)
    plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = I
end
function BTP(I)
    local e = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    local K = e.Character.HumanoidRootPart
    local n = e.Character.Humanoid
    local d = e.PlayerGui.Main
    local z = I.Position
    local H = K.Position
    repeat
        n.Health = 0
        K.CFrame = I
        d.Quest.Visible = false
        if (K.Position - H).Magnitude > 1 then
            H = K.Position
            K.CFrame = I
        end
        task.wait(0.5)
    until (I.Position - K.Position).Magnitude <= 2000
end

QuestBeta = function()
    local I = QuestB()
    return {
        [0] = _G.FindBoss,
        [1] = bMon,
        [2] = Qdata,
        [3] = Qname,
        [4] = PosB,
    }
end

            PosQ = CFrame.new(9640.08789, -1992.44507, 9613.65234, -0.957327187, 4.11991223e-8, 0.289006323, 1.5775445e-8, 1, -9.02985846E-8, -0.289006323, -8.18860855E-8, -0.957327187)
            if (getgenv().AutoFarm or _G.Level) and (PosQ.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > 10000 then
                _tp(CFrame.new(-16269.7041, 25.2288494, 1373.65955, 0.997390985, 1.47309942e-9, -7.21890926E-2, -4.00651912E-9, 0.99999994, -2.51183763E-9, 0.0721890852, 5.75363091e-10, 0.997390926))
                task.wait(2)
                local args = {
                    'TravelToSubmergedIsland',
                }
                game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Modules.Net:FindFirstChild('RF/SubmarineWorkerSpeak'):InvokeServer(unpack(args))
                return
            end
            Mon = 'Grand Devotee'
            Qdata = 2
            Qname = 'SubmergedQuest3'
            NameMon = 'Grand Devotee'
            PosM = CFrame.new(9611.70508, -1993.47119, 9882.68848, -0.591375351, 4.14332426e-8, -0.806396425, 4.73774868e-8, 1, 1.66361875e-8, 0.806396425, -2.83668058E-8, -0.591375351)
        end
    end
end
MaterialMon = function()
    local I = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    local e = I.Character and I.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
    if not e then
        return
    end
    shouldRequestEntrance = function(I, K)
        local n = (e.Position - I).Magnitude
        if n >= K then
            replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', I)
        end
    end
    if World1 then
        if SelectMaterial == 'Angel Wings' then
            MMon = {
                'Shanda',
                'Royal Squad',
                'Royal Soldier',
                'Wysper',
                'Thunder God',
            }
            MPos = CFrame.new(-4698, 845, -1912)
            SP = 'Default'
            local I = Vector3.new(-4607.82275, 872.54248, -1667.55688)
            shouldRequestEntrance(I, 10000)
        elseif SelectMaterial == 'Leather + Scrap Metal' then
            MMon = {
                'Brute',
                'Pirate',
            }
            MPos = CFrame.new(-1145, 15, 4350)
            SP = 'Default'
        elseif SelectMaterial == 'Magma Ore' then
            MMon = {
                'Military Soldier',
                'Military Spy',
                'Magma Admiral',
            }
            MPos = CFrame.new(-5815, 84, 8820)
            SP = 'Default'
        elseif SelectMaterial == 'Fish Tail' then
            MMon = {
                'Fishman Warrior',
                'Fishman Commando',
                'Fishman Lord',
            }
            MPos = CFrame.new(61123, 19, 1569)
            SP = 'Default'
            local I = Vector3.new(61163.8515625, 5.342342376709, 1819.7841796875)
            shouldRequestEntrance(I, 17000)
        end
    elseif World2 then
        if SelectMaterial == 'Leather + Scrap Metal' then
            MMon = {
                'Marine Captain',
            }
            MPos = CFrame.new(-2010.5059814453, 73.001159667969, -3326.6208496094)
            SP = 'Default'
        elseif SelectMaterial == 'Magma Ore' then
            MMon = {
                'Magma Ninja',
                'Lava Pirate',
            }
            MPos = CFrame.new(-5428, 78, -5959)
            SP = 'Default'
        elseif SelectMaterial == 'Ectoplasm' then
            MMon = {
                'Ship Deckhand',
                'Ship Engineer',
                'Ship Steward',
                'Ship Officer',
            }
            MPos = CFrame.new(911.35827636719, 125.95812988281, 33159.5390625)
            SP = 'Default'
            local I = Vector3.new(923.21252441406, 126.9760055542, 32852.83203125)
            shouldRequestEntrance(I, 1000)
        elseif SelectMaterial == 'Mystic Droplet' then
            MMon = {
                'Water Fighter',
            }
            MPos = CFrame.new(-3385, 239, -10542)
            SP = 'Default'
        elseif SelectMaterial == 'Radioactive Material' then
            MMon = {
                'Factory Staff',
            }
            MPos = CFrame.new(295, 73, -56)
            SP = 'Default'
        elseif SelectMaterial == 'Vampire Fang' then
            MMon = {
                'Vampire',
            }
            MPos = CFrame.new(-6033, 7, -1317)
            SP = 'Default'
        end
    elseif World3 then
        if SelectMaterial == 'Scrap Metal' then
            MMon = {
                'Jungle Pirate',
                'Forest Pirate',
            }
            MPos = CFrame.new(-11975.78515625, 331.77340698242, -10620.030273438)
            SP = 'Default'
        elseif SelectMaterial == 'Fish Tail' then
            MMon = {
                'Fishman Raider',
                'Fishman Captain',
            }
            MPos = CFrame.new(-10993, 332, -8940)
            SP = 'Default'
        elseif SelectMaterial == 'Conjured Cocoa' then
            MMon = {
                'Chocolate Bar Battler',
                'Cocoa Warrior',
            }
            MPos = CFrame.new(620.63446044922, 78.936447143555, -12581.369140625)
            SP = 'Default'
        elseif SelectMaterial == 'Dragon Scale' then
            MMon = {
                'Dragon Crew Archer',
                'Dragon Crew Warrior',
            }
            MPos = CFrame.new(6594, 383, 139)
            SP = 'Default'
        elseif SelectMaterial == 'Gunpowder' then
            MMon = {

            SP = 'Default'

GetQuestData = function()
    local I = QuestCheck()
    return {
        [1] = Mon,
        [2] = Qdata,
        [3] = Qname,
        [4] = PosM,
        [5] = NameMon,
        [6] = PosQ,
    }
end

local function TR(key)
    local t = _T[_SelectedLang] or _T['EN']
    return t[key] or (_T['EN'][key] or key)
end

local function ApplyLanguage(newLang)
    if newLang == _SelectedLang then return end
    local oldT = _T[_SelectedLang] or _T['EN']
    local newT = _T[newLang] or _T['EN']
    -- reverse map: oldText → key
    local reverseOld = {}
    for k, v in pairs(oldT) do
        if type(v) == 'string' then
            reverseOld[v] = k
        end
    end
    _SelectedLang = newLang
    _G.Language = newLang
    -- scan all GUI text elements and swap matching text
    pcall(function()
        for _, obj in ipairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA('TextLabel') or obj:IsA('TextButton') or obj:IsA('TextBox') then
                local key = reverseOld[obj.Text]
                if key then
                    local newText = newT[key] or oldT[key]
                    if newText then
                        obj.Text = newText
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end
local _LoadGui = Instance.new('ScreenGui')
_LoadGui.Name = 'SEIHubLoading'
_LoadGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
_LoadGui.DisplayOrder = 999
_LoadGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
pcall(function() _LoadGui.Parent = game:GetService('CoreGui') end)
if not _LoadGui.Parent then _LoadGui.Parent = game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui end

-- Background: deep ocean-night gradient
local _LoadFrame = Instance.new('Frame', _LoadGui)
_LoadFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
_LoadFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(6, 10, 22)
_LoadFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
local _BgGrad = Instance.new('UIGradient', _LoadFrame)
_BgGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(10, 16, 34)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, Color3.fromRGB(6, 10, 22)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(3, 5, 12)),
})
_BgGrad.Rotation = 90

-- Scattered "stars" for depth
local _StarHolder = Instance.new('Frame', _LoadFrame)
_StarHolder.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
_StarHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
for i = 1, 36 do
    local star = Instance.new('Frame', _StarHolder)
    local sz = math.random(2, 3)
    star.Size = UDim2.new(0, sz, 0, sz)
    star.Position = UDim2.new(math.random(0, 1000) / 1000, 0, math.random(0, 1000) / 1000, 0)
    star.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    star.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - (math.random(2, 6) / 10)
    star.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Instance.new('UICorner', star).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(math.random() * 2)
        while star.Parent do
            local tw1 = game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(star, TweenInfo.new(math.random(12, 22) / 10), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.85 })
            tw1:Play(); tw1.Completed:Wait()
            local tw2 = game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(star, TweenInfo.new(math.random(12, 22) / 10), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 - (math.random(2, 6) / 10) })
            tw2:Play(); tw2.Completed:Wait()
        end
    end)
end

local function _SetProgress(pct, animated)
    pcall(function()
        local target = UDim2.new(math.clamp(pct, 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
        if animated then
            game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(_Fill, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Size = target }):Play()
        else
            _Fill.Size = target
        end
    end)
end

local function _SetStatus(text)
    pcall(function()
        local tw = game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(_LoadSub, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { TextTransparency = 1 })
        tw:Play()
        tw.Completed:Wait()
        _LoadSub.Text = text
        game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(_LoadSub, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { TextTransparency = 0 }):Play()
    end)
end

-- ปิด loading screen เสมอ ไม่ว่าจะโหลดสำเร็จหรือไม่
task.delay(0.5, function()
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local tweens = {}
        table.insert(tweens, game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(_Center, TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Position = _Center.Position + UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, -14) }))
        for _, obj in ipairs(_Center:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA('TextLabel') then
                pcall(function()
                    table.insert(tweens, game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(obj, TweenInfo.new(0.4), { TextTransparency = 1 }))
                end)
            elseif obj:IsA('ImageLabel') then
                pcall(function()
                    table.insert(tweens, game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(obj, TweenInfo.new(0.4), { ImageTransparency = 1 }))
                end)
            elseif obj:IsA('Frame') then
                pcall(function()
                    table.insert(tweens, game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(obj, TweenInfo.new(0.4), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
                end)
            end
            if obj:IsA('GuiObject') then
                pcall(function()
                    local stroke = obj:FindFirstChildOfClass('UIStroke')
                    if stroke then
                        table.insert(tweens, game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(stroke, TweenInfo.new(0.4), { Transparency = 1 }))
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        for _, tw in ipairs(tweens) do
            pcall(function() tw:Play() end)
        end
        local fadeOut = game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(_LoadFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.5), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
        fadeOut:Play()
        fadeOut.Completed:Wait()
    end)
    -- Always remove the loading GUI, even if a tween above failed
    pcall(function()
        if _LoadGui and _LoadGui.Parent then
            _LoadGui:Destroy()
        end
    end)
end)

    end)
    if not ok2 or not Fluent then
        warn('[SEI Hub] Fluent UI load failed permanently. Script cannot continue.')
        return
    end
end

local _InfoParagraphs = {
    timezone   = Status:AddParagraph({ Title = 'เขตเวลา',             Description = '...' }),
    servertime = Status:AddParagraph({ Title = 'เวลา',                Description = '...' }),
    mirage     = Status:AddParagraph({ Title = 'Mirage Island',        Description = '...' }),
    kitsune    = Status:AddParagraph({ Title = 'Kitsune Island',       Description = '...' }),
    prehistoric= Status:AddParagraph({ Title = 'เกาะยุคดึกดำบรรพ์',   Description = '...' }),
    frozen     = Status:AddParagraph({ Title = 'มิติแช่แข็ง',          Description = '...' }),
    level      = Status:AddParagraph({ Title = 'เลเวล',               Description = '...' }),
    beli       = Status:AddParagraph({ Title = 'เบลี่',               Description = '...' }),
    fragment   = Status:AddParagraph({ Title = 'Fragment',             Description = '...' }),
    race       = Status:AddParagraph({ Title = 'เผ่า',                Description = '...' }),
    world      = Status:AddParagraph({ Title = 'World',               Description = '...' }),
    jobid      = Status:AddParagraph({ Title = 'Job ID',              Description = '...' }),
}
local function _setPara(para, txt)
    pcall(function() para:SetDesc(txt) end)
end
local function _islandStatus(name)
    local loc = workspace:FindFirstChild('_WorldOrigin') and
                workspace._WorldOrigin:FindFirstChild('Locations')
    if loc and loc:FindFirstChild(name) then
        return '✅ Spawned'
    end
    local map = workspace:FindFirstChild('Map')
    if name == 'MysticIsland' and map and map:FindFirstChild('MysticIsland') then
        return '✅ Spawned'
    end
    return '❌ Not Spawned'
end
local function _frozenStatus()
    local map = workspace:FindFirstChild('Map')
    return (map and map:FindFirstChild('FrozenDimension')) and '✅ Active' or '❌ Not Active'
end
local function _prehistoricStatus()
    local map = workspace:FindFirstChild('Map')
    return (map and map:FindFirstChild('PrehistoricIsland')) and '✅ Spawned' or '❌ Not Spawned'
end
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(0.55) 
    while task.wait(2) do
        pcall(function()
            local lp = game.Players.LocalPlayer
            local char = lp.Character
            local gt = math.floor(workspace.DistributedGameTime)
            local h = math.floor(gt / 3600) % 24
            local m = math.floor(gt / 60) % 60
            local s = gt % 60
            _setPara(_InfoParagraphs.servertime,
                string.format('%02d:%02d:%02d (ตั้งแต่เซิร์ฟเปิด)', h, m, s))
            local timeStr = os.date('%H:%M:%S')
            _setPara(_InfoParagraphs.timezone, tostring(timeStr) .. ' (เวลาเซิร์ฟเวอร์)')
            _setPara(_InfoParagraphs.mirage,      _islandStatus('MysticIsland'))
            _setPara(_InfoParagraphs.kitsune,     _islandStatus('Kitsune Island'))
            _setPara(_InfoParagraphs.prehistoric, _prehistoricStatus())
            _setPara(_InfoParagraphs.frozen,      _frozenStatus())
            local data = lp:FindFirstChild('Data')
            if data then
                local lvl      = data:FindFirstChild('Level')      and data.Level.Value      or '?'
                local beli     = data:FindFirstChild('Beli')        and data.Beli.Value       or '?'
                local frag     = data:FindFirstChild('Fragments')   and data.Fragments.Value  or '?'
                local raceObj  = data:FindFirstChild('Race')
                local raceVal  = raceObj and (raceObj.Value or tostring(raceObj)) or '?'
                _setPara(_InfoParagraphs.level,    tostring(lvl))
                _setPara(_InfoParagraphs.beli,     '$' .. tostring(beli))
                _setPara(_InfoParagraphs.fragment, tostring(frag) .. ' F')
                _setPara(_InfoParagraphs.race,     tostring(raceVal))
            end
            local worldName = World1 and 'Sea 1' or World2 and 'Sea 2' or World3 and 'Sea 3' or 'Unknown'
            _setPara(_InfoParagraphs.world, worldName)
            _setPara(_InfoParagraphs.jobid, tostring(game.JobId))
        end)
    end
end)

-- ============================================================
-- Helper: บินไปจุดซื้อ → รัน rebuy ระหว่างบิน → หยุดเมื่อถึง
-- ============================================================
local _FightBuyActive = false

local function _GetFightingStyleTarget(styleName)
    -- คืน CFrame ปลายทางตามโลกที่ผู้เล่นอยู่
    local targets = {
        ['BlackLeg'] = {
            Sea1 = CFrame.new(-986.217346, 13.814609, 3989.008545),
            Sea2 = CFrame.new(-4753.985352, 35.104927, -4850.409180),
            Sea3 = CFrame.new(-5043.907715, 371.375519, -3178.755127),
        },
        ['FishmanKarate'] = {
            Sea1 = CFrame.new(61581.949219, 18.933592, 985.699463),
            Sea2 = CFrame.new(-4955.416504, 35.103790, -4667.795410),
            Sea3 = CFrame.new(-5022.497070, 371.423157, -3187.188721),
        },
        ['Electro'] = {
            Sea1 = CFrame.new(-5386.339355, 13.713322, -2148.025635),
            Sea2 = CFrame.new(-4864.149902, 35.104767, -4766.276855),
            Sea3 = CFrame.new(-4994.052246, 314.578491, -3202.991211),
        },
        ['DragonBreath'] = {
            Sea2 = CFrame.new(702.345886, 187.022858, 654.894897),
            Sea3 = CFrame.new(-4980.071777, 371.375580, -3206.102539),
        },
        ['SharkmanKarate'] = {
            Sea2 = CFrame.new(-2602.075928, 239.413712, -10316.080078),
            Sea3 = CFrame.new(-4973.455078, 314.578491, -3222.074951),
        },
        ['ElectricClaw'] = {
            Sea3 = CFrame.new(-10369.771484, 331.716736, -10129.354492),
        },
        ['DragonTalon'] = {
            Sea3 = CFrame.new(5662.412109, 1211.340332, 865.085938),
        },
        ['GodHuman'] = {
            Sea3 = CFrame.new(-13771.823242, 334.685089, -9880.160156),
        },
        ['SanguineArt'] = {
            Sea3 = CFrame.new(-16514.400391, 23.202719, -190.464355),
        },
    }

    local styleTargets = targets[styleName]
    if not styleTargets then return nil end

    if World1 and styleTargets.Sea1 then
        return styleTargets.Sea1
    elseif World2 and styleTargets.Sea2 then
        return styleTargets.Sea2
    elseif World3 and styleTargets.Sea3 then
        return styleTargets.Sea3
    end
    return nil
end

local function _RunFightingStyleBuy(styleName, buyFunc)
    -- ถ้ากำลังทำงานอยู่แล้ว ให้หยุดก่อน
    if _FightBuyActive then
        _FightBuyActive = false
        task.wait(0.3)
    end

    local targetCF = _GetFightingStyleTarget(styleName)
    if not targetCF then
        Fluent:Notify({ Title = 'SEI Hub', Content = styleName .. ' ไม่รองรับในโลกนี้!', Duration = 4 })
        return
    end

    _FightBuyActive = true
    Fluent:Notify({ Title = 'SEI Hub', Content = 'กำลังบินไปซื้อ ' .. styleName .. '...', Duration = 3 })

    task.spawn(function()
        local Player = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer

        -- ---- รัน rebuy ระหว่างบิน (loop ในอีก thread) ----
        local buyThread = task.spawn(function()
            while _FightBuyActive do
                pcall(buyFunc)
                task.wait(0.5)
            end
        end)

        -- ---- บินไปยังเป้าหมาย ----
        local char = Player.Character
        local HRP  = char and char:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
        if not HRP then
            _FightBuyActive = false
            task.cancel(buyThread)
            return
        end

        -- เปิด flag → master loop (line ~1714) จะจัดการ BodyClip + noclip ให้เอง
        -- เหมือนกับที่ _G.AutoFarm / _G.Level ทำ
        _G.FightStyleBuy = true
        task.wait()  -- รอ 1 tick ให้ master loop เปิด BodyClip ก่อน

        -- บิน tween ไปยังเป้าหมาย
        -- ถ้าถูกเกมดึงกลางทาง (PlaybackState = Cancelled) → anchor ลอยค้างตรงนั้น แล้วบินต่อ
        local TweenService = game:GetService('TweenService')
        while _FightBuyActive do
            char = Player.Character
            HRP  = char and char:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
            if not HRP then break end

            local dist = (HRP.Position - targetCF.Position).Magnitude
            if dist < 20 then break end

            local speed = getgenv().TweenSpeedFar or 370
            local tw = TweenService:Create(
                HRP,
                TweenInfo.new(dist / speed, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),
                { CFrame = targetCF }
            )
            tw:Play()
            local state = tw.Completed:Wait()

            if state == Enum.PlaybackState.Cancelled then
                -- เกมดึงกลางทาง → หยุดลอยค้างตรงนั้นก่อน แล้วบินต่อ
                char = Player.Character
                HRP  = char and char:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if HRP then
                    HRP.Anchored = true
                    task.wait(0.35)
                    HRP.Anchored = false
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
                -- วนกลับขึ้นไป tween ใหม่จากตำแหน่งปัจจุบัน
            end
            -- state = Completed → วน loop เช็ค dist อีกรอบ (ถ้ายังไม่ถึงก็ tween ต่อ)
        end

        -- ---- ถึงแล้ว: หยุดทุกอย่าง ----
        _FightBuyActive = false
        task.cancel(buyThread)

        -- ปิด flag → master loop จะปิด BodyClip + noclip ให้เองใน tick ถัดไป
        _G.FightStyleBuy = false

        -- ซื้อครั้งสุดท้ายเมื่อถึงที่
        pcall(buyFunc)

        Fluent:Notify({ Title = 'SEI Hub', Content = 'ซื้อ ' .. styleName .. ' เสร็จแล้ว!', Duration = 4 })
    end)
end

    Callback = function(I)

        for _, e in pairs(plr.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
            if e:IsA('Tool') and e.ToolTip == _G.ChooseWP then
                _G.SelectWeapon = e.Name
                break
            end
        end
        local charTool = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
        if charTool and charTool.ToolTip == _G.ChooseWP then
            _G.SelectWeapon = charTool.Name
        end
    end,
})
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(0.7) 
    while task.wait(Sec) do
        pcall(function()
            for _, e in pairs(plr.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
                if e:IsA('Tool') and e.ToolTip == _G.ChooseWP then
                    if plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(e.Name) then
                        _G.SelectWeapon = e.Name
                    end
                end
            end
            if not _G.SelectWeapon then
                local charTool = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
                if charTool and charTool.ToolTip == _G.ChooseWP then
                    _G.SelectWeapon = charTool.Name
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)

local CurrentMobLevel = nil
local CurrentMobLevelSystem = nil
local TELEPORT_DISTANCE_THRESHOLD = 15
function TeleportConditional(hrp, targetCFrame, threshold)
    if not hrp or not targetCFrame then
        return
    end
    local dist = (targetCFrame.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
    if dist > threshold then
        _tp(targetCFrame)
    end
end
_G.FastAttack = true  -- default เปิด
Farm:AddToggle('ToggleFastAttack', {
    Title = 'Fast Attack',
    Default = true,  -- เปิดตั้งแต่เริ่ม กดปิดได้ปกติ
    Callback = function(I)
        _G.FastAttack = I
        _G.SaveData.AutoAttack_Save = I
        SaveSettings()
        _G.Seriality = I
        _G.NewAutoAttack = I
    end,
})
do
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local ReplicatedStorage = game.ReplicatedStorage
    local Enemies = game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local Modules = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Modules")
    local Net = require(Modules:WaitForChild("Net"))
    local RegisterAttack = Net:RemoteEvent("RegisterAttack", true)
    local RegisterHit = Net:RemoteEvent("RegisterHit", true)
    local AttackDistance = 120
    local function getClosestEnemy(distance)
        local target = nil
        local others = {}
        for _, enemy in pairs(Enemies:GetChildren()) do
            local hrp = enemy:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                local dist = LocalPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(hrp.Position)
                if dist < distance then
                    if not target then target = hrp
                    else table.insert(others, {enemy, hrp}) end
                end
            end
        end
        for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Character then
                local hrp = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp then
                    local dist = LocalPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(hrp.Position)
                    if dist < distance then
                        if not target then target = hrp
                        else table.insert(others, {player, hrp}) end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return target, others
    end
    _G.NewAutoAttack = false
    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not _G.NewAutoAttack then return end
        pcall(function()
            local target, others = getClosestEnemy(AttackDistance)
            if target then
                RegisterAttack:FireServer(0)
                RegisterHit:FireServer(target, others)
            end
        end)
    end)
end

local FarmHeight = 30
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(1) 
    game:GetService('RunService').Stepped:Connect(function()
        if _G.AutoTyrant and _G.StartFarm then
            local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
            if char then
                for _, part in pairs(char:GetDescendants())do
                    if part:IsA('BasePart') and part.CanCollide then
                        part.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
                if char:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') then
                    char.Humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                    char.Humanoid:ChangeState(11)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end)
function GetTargetByLevel()
    local myLevel = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Data.Level.Value
    if myLevel >= 2575 then
        return {
            Name = 'Skull Slayer',
            QuestArgs = {
                'StartQuest',
                'TikiQuest3',
                2,
            },
            QuestPos = CFrame.new(-16665.0879, 105.27478, 1577.61743, -0.999621451, 3.5280582000000003e-8, 0.0275127869, 3.5990368e-8, 1, 2.53032191e-8, -2.75127869E-2, 2.62838356e-8, -0.999621451),
            FarmPos = CFrame.new(-16709.49, 419.68, 1751.09),
        }
    elseif myLevel > 2550 then
        return {
            Name = 'Serpent Hunter',
            QuestArgs = {
                'StartQuest',
                'TikiQuest3',
                1,
            },

            FarmPos = CFrame.new(-16645.64, 163.09, 1352.87),
        }

        return {

            QuestArgs = {
                'StartQuest',
                'TikiQuest2',
                2,
            },
            QuestPos = CFrame.new(-16546.748, 55.7216759, -172.865311, -5.95058464E-2, 2.45485676e-8, 0.998227954, -5.3272025E-8, 1, -2.77677703E-8, -0.998227954, -5.48299717E-8, -5.95058464E-2),

            QuestPos = CFrame.new(-16546.748, 55.7216759, -172.865311, -5.95058464E-2, 2.45485676e-8, 0.998227954, -5.3272025E-8, 1, -2.77677703E-8, -0.998227954, -5.48299717E-8, -5.95058464E-2),
