-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════
-- SEI Hub Attack System (No loadstring)
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════
local _RS     = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')
local _Players = game:GetService('Players')
local _Run    = game:GetService('RunService')
local _LP     = _Players.LocalPlayer
local _Net    = _RS:WaitForChild('Modules'):WaitForChild('Net')
local _RegAtk = _Net:WaitForChild('RE/RegisterAttack')
local _RegHit = _Net:WaitForChild('RE/RegisterHit')
local _ShootEv= _Net:WaitForChild('RE/ShootGunEvent')

local ATK = {}
ATK.Distance   = 65
ATK.GunDist    = 120
ATK.Cooldown   = 0.1
ATK.GunDelay   = 0.1
ATK._debounce  = 0
ATK._gunDebounce = 0
ATK._combo     = 0
ATK._comboTime = 0
ATK.MaxCombo   = 4
ATK.ComboReset = 0.35

-- หาศัตรูในระยะ (Enemies + Characters ยกเว้นตัวเอง)
function ATK:GetTargets(dist)
    local char = _LP.Character
    if not char then return {} end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
    if not hrp then return {} end
    local pos = hrp.Position
    local results = {}
    local folders = {workspace.Enemies}
    if workspace:FindFirstChild('Characters') then
        table.insert(folders, workspace.Characters)
    end
    for _, folder in ipairs(folders) do
        for _, e in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
            if e ~= char then
                local eh = e:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
                local ep = e:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if eh and ep and eh.Health > 0 then
                    local d = (ep.Position - pos).Magnitude
                    if d <= (dist or self.Distance) then
                        table.insert(results, e)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return results
end

-- หาศัตรูที่ใกล้ที่สุด
function ATK:GetNearest(dist)
    local char = _LP.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
    if not hrp then return nil end
    local pos = hrp.Position
    local nearest, nearDist = nil, math.huge
    local folders = {workspace.Enemies}
    if workspace:FindFirstChild('Characters') then
        table.insert(folders, workspace.Characters)
    end
    for _, folder in ipairs(folders) do
        for _, e in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
            if e ~= char then
                local eh = e:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
                local ep = e:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if eh and ep and eh.Health > 0 then
                    local d = (ep.Position - pos).Magnitude
                    if d <= (dist or self.Distance) and d < nearDist then
                        nearest = e
                        nearDist = d
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nearest
end

-- Combo counter
function ATK:NextCombo()
    local now = tick()
    if now - self._comboTime > self.ComboReset then
        self._combo = 0
    end
    self._combo = (self._combo % self.MaxCombo) + 1
    self._comboTime = now
    return self._combo
end

-- เช็คว่าตัวละครพร้อมตีไหม
function ATK:CanAttack(char, hum)
    if hum.Sit then return false end
    local stun = char:FindFirstChild('Stun')
    local busy = char:FindFirstChild('Busy')
    if stun and stun.Value > 0 then return false end
    if busy and busy.Value then return false end
    return true
end

-- ตีปกติ (Sword/Melee) — ยิง RegisterAttack + RegisterHit
function ATK:MeleeAttack(targets, combo)
    if #targets == 0 then return end
    local first = targets[1]
    local firstHRP = first:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
    if not firstHRP then return end
    local cd = combo >= self.MaxCombo and 0.35 or self.Cooldown
    pcall(function() _RegAtk:FireServer(cd) end)
    local hitArgs = { firstHRP, {} }
    for i, e in ipairs(targets) do
        local ep = e:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
        if ep then
            hitArgs[2][i] = { e, ep }
        end
    end
    pcall(function() _RegHit:FireServer(unpack(hitArgs)) end)
end

-- ตีผลไม้ M1 (Blox Fruit LeftClickRemote)
function ATK:FruitM1(eq, nearest, combo)
    local remote = eq:FindFirstChild('LeftClickRemote')
    if not remote or not nearest then return end
    local char = _LP.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
    local ep  = nearest:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
    if not hrp or not ep then return end
    local dir = (ep.Position - hrp.Position).Unit
    pcall(function() remote:FireServer(Vector3.new(dir.X, dir.Y, dir.Z), combo) end)
end

-- ยิงปืน
function ATK:GunAttack(nearest)
    if not nearest then return end
    local ep = nearest:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
    if not ep then return end
    local now = tick()
    if now - self._gunDebounce < self.GunDelay then return end
    self._gunDebounce = now
    -- หา handle ของศัตรู
    local handle = ep
    for _, d in ipairs(nearest:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA('BasePart') and d.Name == 'Handle' then
            handle = d
            break
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        _ShootEv:FireServer(
            Vector3.new(ep.Position.X, ep.Position.Y, ep.Position.Z),
            { handle }
        )
    end)
end

-- Attack หลัก เรียกทุก tick
function ATK:Attack()
    if not _G.AutoClickAttack then return end
    local now = tick()
    if now - self._debounce < self.Cooldown then return end

    local char = _LP.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')
    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    if not self:CanAttack(char, hum) then return end

    local eq = char:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
    if not eq then return end
    local tt = eq.ToolTip

    if tt == 'Sword' or tt == 'Melee' then
        local targets = self:GetTargets()
        if #targets == 0 then return end
        local combo = self:NextCombo()
        local cd = combo >= self.MaxCombo and 0.35 or self.Cooldown
        self._debounce = now + (combo >= self.MaxCombo and 0.35 or 0)
        self:MeleeAttack(targets, combo)

    elseif tt == 'Blox Fruit' then
        local nearest = self:GetNearest()
        if not nearest then return end
        local combo = self:NextCombo()
        self._debounce = now
        self:FruitM1(eq, nearest, combo)

    elseif tt == 'Gun' then
        local nearest = self:GetNearest(self.GunDist)
        if not nearest then return end
        self._debounce = now
        self:GunAttack(nearest)
    end
end

-- Main loop
_Run.Stepped:Connect(function()
    pcall(function() ATK:Attack() end)
end)

Time = Status:AddParagraph({
    Title = TR('time_zone'),
})
function UpdateOS()
    date = os.date('*t')
    hour = (date.hour) % 24
    ampm = hour < 12 and 'AM' or 'PM'
    timezone = string.format('%02i:%02i:%02i %s', ((hour - 1) % 12) + 1, date.min, date.sec, ampm)
    datetime = string.format('%02d/%02d/%04d', date.day, date.month, date.year)
    LocalizationService = game:GetService('LocalizationService')
    Players = game:GetService('Players')
    player = Players.LocalPlayer
    name = player.Name
    result, code = pcall(function()
        return LocalizationService:GetCountryRegionForPlayerAsync(player)
    end)
    Time:SetDesc((datetime .. ' - ' .. timezone .. ' [ ' .. code .. ' ]'))
end
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(3.55) 
    while task.wait() do
        UpdateOS()
        game:GetService('RunService').RenderStepped:Wait()
    end
end)
Timmessss = Status:AddParagraph({
    Title = TR('time'),
})
function UpdateTime()
    GameTime = math.floor(workspace.DistributedGameTime + 0.5)
    Hour = math.floor(GameTime / (36E2)) % 24
    Minute = math.floor(GameTime / 60) % 60
    Second = GameTime % 60
    Timmessss:SetDesc((Hour .. ' Hour (h) ' .. Minute .. ' Minute (m) ' .. Second .. ' Second (s) '))
end
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(3.7) 
    while task.wait() do
        UpdateTime()
        task.wait()
    end
end)

Players = Players or game:GetService("Players")

Player = Player or Players.LocalPlayer
PlaceId = PlaceId or game.PlaceId
JobId = JobId or game.JobId
_G.AutoRejoin30m = false
RejoinRunning = false
function GetNewServer()
    local Servers = {}
    local req = game:HttpGet(
        "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/"..PlaceId.."/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"
    )
    local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(req)
    for _, server in pairs(data.data) do
        if server.playing < server.maxPlayers and server.id ~= JobId then
            table.insert(Servers, server.id)
        end
    end
    if #Servers > 0 then
        return Servers[math.random(1, #Servers)]
    end
end

    Title = TR('btn_antireset'),
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        _G.AutoRejoin30m = Value
        if Value and not RejoinRunning then
            RejoinRunning = true
            task.spawn(function()
                while _G.AutoRejoin30m do
                    task.wait(1800) 
                    if not _G.AutoRejoin30m then break end
                    local NewServer = GetNewServer()
                    if NewServer then
                        TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(PlaceId, NewServer, Player)
                    else
                        TeleportService:Teleport(PlaceId, Player)
                    end
                end
                RejoinRunning = false
            end)
        end
    end
})

Status:AddButton({
    Title = TR('btn_hop'),
    Callback = function()
        local Http = game:GetService("HttpService")
        local ServerBrowser = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("__ServerBrowser")
        local url = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/"..game.PlaceId.."/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"
        local success, data = pcall(function()
            return Http:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet(url))
        end)
        if not success or not data or not data.data then
            return warn("Failed to fetch servers")
        end
        local target = nil
        for _, server in pairs(data.data) do
            if server and server.playing and server.maxPlayers then
                if server.playing < server.maxPlayers then
                    target = server
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if not target then
            return warn("No server found")
        end
        for i = 1, 3 do
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function()
                    ServerBrowser:InvokeServer("teleport", target.id)
                end)
            end)
        end
    end,
})
Status:AddButton({
    Title = TR('btn_hop_less'),
    Callback = function()
        local Http = game:GetService("HttpService")
        local ServerBrowser = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("__ServerBrowser")
        local Api = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/"
        local placeId = game.PlaceId
        local baseUrl = Api .. placeId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"
        local lowestServer = nil
        local cursor = ""
        function ListServers(cursor)
            local success, result = pcall(function()
                local raw = game:HttpGet(baseUrl .. (cursor ~= "" and "&cursor=" .. cursor or ""))
                return Http:JSONDecode(raw)
            end)
            if success and result and result.data then
                return result
            end
            return nil
        end
        for i = 1, 5 do
            local data = ListServers(cursor)
            if not data then
                warn("Fetch failed, retrying...")
                task.wait(1)
                continue
            end
            for _, server in pairs(data.data) do
                if server and server.playing and server.maxPlayers then
                    if server.playing < server.maxPlayers then
                        if not lowestServer or server.playing < lowestServer.playing then
                            lowestServer = server
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            cursor = data.nextPageCursor or ""
            if cursor == "" then break end
        end
        if not lowestServer then
            return warn("No server found")
        end
        for i = 1, 5 do
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function()
                    ServerBrowser:InvokeServer("teleport", lowestServer.id)
                end)
            end)
        end
    end,
})

})

local CharacterHighlight = nil 
local function SetHighlight(fillColor, outlineColor)
    local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    if CharacterHighlight and CharacterHighlight.Parent then
        CharacterHighlight:Destroy()
    end
    if fillColor == nil then
        CharacterHighlight = nil
        return
    end
    local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
    hl.FillColor     = fillColor
    hl.OutlineColor  = outlineColor or fillColor
    hl.FillTransparency = 0.5
    hl.OutlineTransparency = 0.0   
    hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop  
    hl.Parent = char
    CharacterHighlight = hl
end
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(0.25) 
    while task.wait(0.3) do
        pcall(function()
            local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
            if not char then return end
            if CharacterHighlight and CharacterHighlight.Parent ~= char then
                CharacterHighlight = nil
            end
            if IsFarmActive()
                or _G.AutoBoss or _G.FarmAllBoss or _G.DungeonFarm
                or _G.Fly or _G.StartFarm
                or _G.AutoFarmNear or _G.AutoFarm_Bone or _G.AutoFarm_Cake
                or _G.AutoTyrant or _G.obsFarm
                or _G.FarmMastery_G or _G.FarmMastery_Dev or _G.FarmMastery_S
                or _G.SeaBeast1 or _G.Leviathan1 or _G.FishBoat or _G.PGB
                or _G.AutoRaidCastle or _G.AutoFarmRaid or _G.Raiding
                or _G.AutoEcBoss or _G.AutoBigmom or _G.AuraBoss
                or _G.CDK or _G.CDK_TS or _G.CDK_YM
                or _G.Auto_Soul_Guitar or _G.AutoFistDarkness
                or _G.beasthunter or _G.Lvthan or _G.TerrorShark
                or _G.tweenKitsune or _G.TweenToKitsuneIsland
                or _G.TweenToPrehistoricIsland or _G.SummonPrehistoricIsland
                or _G.TweenToFrozenDimension or _G.TweenToMirageIsland then
                SetHighlight(
                    Color3.fromRGB(0, 150, 255),
                    Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                )
            else
                SetHighlight(nil)
            end
        end)
    end
end)
game.Players.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    CharacterHighlight = nil  
end)

function AutoHaki()
    if not game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HasBuso") then
        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Buso")
    end
end
function UnEquipWeapon(Weapon)
    if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(Weapon) then
        _G.NotAutoEquip = true
        wait(.1)
        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(Weapon).Parent = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack
        wait(.1)
        _G.NotAutoEquip = false
    end
end
function TelePPlayer(P)
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = P
end
function TPB(CFgo)
    local tween_s = game:service"TweenService"
    local info = TweenInfo.new((game:GetService("Workspace").Boats.PirateBrigade.VehicleSeat.CFrame.Position - CFgo.Position).Magnitude/300, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
    tween = tween_s:Create(game:GetService("Workspace").Boats.PirateBrigade.VehicleSeat, info, {CFrame = CFgo})
    tween:Play()
    local tweenfunc = {}
    function tweenfunc:Stop()
        tween:Cancel()
    end
    return tweenfunc
end
function TPP(CFgo)
    if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid").Health <= 0 or not game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid") then
        tween:Cancel()
        repeat wait() until game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid") and game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid").Health > 0
        wait(7)
        return
    end
    local tween_s = game:service"TweenService"
    local info = TweenInfo.new((game:GetService("Players")["LocalPlayer"].Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - CFgo.Position).Magnitude/325, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
    tween = tween_s:Create(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character["HumanoidRootPart"], info, {CFrame = CFgo})
    tween:Play()
    local tweenfunc = {}
    function tweenfunc:Stop()
        tween:Cancel()
    end
    return tweenfunc
end
function ATween(Pos)
    local Player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    local Character = Player.Character
    local HRP = Character.HumanoidRootPart
    local Humanoid = Character.Humanoid
    local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local Distance = (Pos.Position - HRP.Position).Magnitude
    if Humanoid.Sit then
        Humanoid.Sit = false
    end
    local Speed
    if Distance <= 200 then
        Speed = 99999
    elseif Distance <= 500 then
        Speed = 325
    else
        Speed = 255
    end
    local tween
    pcall(function()
        tween = TweenService:Create(
            HRP,
            TweenInfo.new(Distance / Speed, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),
            {CFrame = Pos}
        )
    end)
    tween:Play()
    if Distance <= 10 then
        tween:Cancel()
        HRP.CFrame = Pos
    end
    if _G.StopTween then
        tween:Cancel()
        _G.Clip = false
    end
end
function StopTween(target)
    local Player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    local Character = Player.Character
    local HRP = Character.HumanoidRootPart
    if not target then
        _G.StopTween = true
        wait()
        ATween(HRP.CFrame)
        wait()
        local BodyClip = HRP:FindFirstChild("BodyClip")
        if BodyClip then
            BodyClip:Destroy()
        end
        _G.StopTween = false
        _G.Clip = false
    end
    local Highlight = Character:FindFirstChild("Highlight")
    if Highlight then
        Highlight:Destroy()
    end
end
spawn(function()
    game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if _G.AutoFarm or _G.AutoSecondSea or _G.AutoThirdSea or _G.AutoFarmBoss or _G.AutoFarmMob or _G.Clip or _G.FightStyleBuy then
            if not game:GetService("Workspace"):FindFirstChild("LOL") then
                local LOL = Instance.new("Part")
                LOL.Name = "LOL"
                LOL.Parent = game.Workspace
                LOL.Anchored = true
                LOL.Transparency = 1
                LOL.Size = Vector3.new(30,-0.5,30)
            elseif game:GetService("Workspace"):FindFirstChild("LOL") then
                game.Workspace["LOL"].CFrame = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -3.6, 0)
            end
        else
            if game:GetService("Workspace"):FindFirstChild("LOL") then
                game:GetService("Workspace"):FindFirstChild("LOL"):Destroy()
            end
        end
    end)
end)

game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Idled:connect(function()
    -- Anti-AFK: ใช้ jump แทน Button2 เพื่อป้องกันกล้องหมุน
    local hum = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character and game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')
    if hum then hum.Jump = true end
end)
